# Tests porting inventory

## Current section

Current checked-in named tests have qualified source identities and file/module scopes. Legacy upstream fixtures plus compiler-owned tests remain separate; file gates, line gates, and fixture metadata affect runtime discovery, which is still unverified.

## Intended section

- source: identical-with-adapter
- behavior: identical-with-aot-rules
- freshness: follow-upstream
- destination: Unchanged current upstream test bodies with engine adapters and separate AOT expectations.
- disposition: required

## Open questions

- Trial current named package tests and preserve their result and error expectations.
- Export actual runner identities and effective exclusions; source discovery is not test execution.

## Named-test discovery

2107 named source declarations in 114 checked-in files.
The category total also includes file groups, backend sources/fixtures, and legacy imports; it is not an executable test count.
IDs use file paths and qualified names, never line numbers. Links use current source lines.
Module scopes use `FILE/named/Qualified/Module`; actual runner discovery, support, and execution remain unverified.
